# Bodyguard S01E01 Selected Sequence Picture Evidence — V0.1

## A. Source and applicability audit

- Evidence ID: `DM-BODYGUARD-S01E01-SEL-001`
- Work / season / episode: source/filename-supplied as *Bodyguard* / Season 1 / Episode 1; not independently established from picture
- Local source handle: `贴身保镖01.mp4`; source media stays local and is not an authored artifact
- Local duration: `00:57:39.167`
- Video basis: 856×480 H.264 picture at exact 24 fps; global file-frame indexing
- Continuous scene: `PARTIAL` — one contiguous selected editorial envelope; the selected start is internal and is not claimed to be the full natural scene start
- Audio basis: one AAC stereo stream is present, but it was not auditioned; every sound claim is `SND-UNK`
- Analyzed range: `[F12635,F31060)` = `00:08:46.458–00:21:34.167` end-exclusive; last retained frame `F31059 / 00:21:34.125`
- Picture limitation: 856×480 resolution supports gross head, hand, body, object and spatial-state observations, not micro-expression certainty. A top-left watermark and lower burned subtitles cover pixels; those pixels and underlying imagery remain `UNKNOWN` and the text was excluded from evidence.
- Why this selected sequence tests the scene problem: `OBSERVED` picture contains a narrow two-person relation, hand/object states, visibly distinct room-like, carriage-like and exterior nodes, a helmeted group at a threshold, optical-like inserts and a later exterior doorway reset. Restricted public-space coordination, negotiation, explosive-device meaning, police roles, remote commands, danger and outcome are not picture facts and remain locked-script-gated or `UNKNOWN`.
- Suitability: `PARTIAL` — suitable for visual coverage, spatial/state continuity and AI fallback candidates; unsuitable for dialogue, sound, role, object-function or causal-story claims without independent locked-script/audio evidence.

### Visual aliases and neutral object handles

`P-A-like`, `P-B-like` and `P-C-like` are anonymous visual-track handles based only on recurring visible hair, wardrobe and body features. `O-ROUND/CABLE`, `O-RED`, `O-METAL`, `O-CYL` and `O-BLUE` name visible form/color only. Within-shot features are `OBSERVED`; cross-cut grouping is `INFERRED`; exact same-person, same-object, same-place, ownership and action continuity remain `UNKNOWN`. Helmeted wardrobe does not establish police, authority or occupation. A green circular reticle-like layer does not establish a weapon, operator, target or real-time view. Any contour overlap is only 2D image overlap unless independently established; physical-contact existence, depth and force remain `UNKNOWN`.

## B. Scene dramatic structure

- Start state — `OBSERVED`: a hard cut enters a wall/sign and door-area view that reframes to a dark-clad upper-body figure (`BODYGUARD-W5-S001`). This is an internal selected start.
- Character objectives — `UNKNOWN`: no negotiation, protection, restraint, disarming, rescue, arrest or escape objective is established from picture alone.
- Obstacle — `UNKNOWN`: close object forms, raised hands, helmeted wardrobe, doorways and optical-like imagery do not prove danger, explosive classification, authority, threat, command or compliance.
- Visible development — `OBSERVED`: repeated person relations, hand/object states, distinct picture nodes, threshold/group positions, 2D overlaps and exterior doorway/body-state changes occur. Their semantic and causal ordering is `INFERRED` or `UNKNOWN`.
- Reaction point — `UNKNOWN`: head, gaze, mouth, hand and posture changes are visible, but no stimulus, dialogue ownership, intention or emotion is proven.
- Turn — `INFERRED` only: the edit increases doorway/group and optical-like alternation around `S093–S171`, then increases hand/object states around `S172–S200`, followed by exterior threshold coverage `S201–S211`. Authority, procedure, success/failure and outcome are `UNKNOWN`.
- End state — `OBSERVED`: `S211` holds a P-A-like exterior close with crossing foreground bodies and a head/body turn before a hard cut to distinct road imagery. Destination, custody, safety and outcome are `UNKNOWN`.
- POV — `INFERRED`: mainly objective editorial coverage plus visible optical-like image inserts. No character/device ownership of the optical layer is verified.

## D. Coverage, spatial design and picture-state phases

- `P0 / S001–S032`: contained relation and body-adjacent object registration.
- `P1 / S033–S065`: visually distinct monitor-lit room-like, desk/display and carriage-occupancy inserts. Remote relation and simultaneity are `UNKNOWN`.
- `P2 / S066–S090`: night exterior rail-vehicle/group imagery alternates with contained views. Exact place, vehicle, chronology and coordination are `UNKNOWN`.
- `P3 / S091–S128`: moving carriage-like registration, doorway/group aperture states, contained reverses and raised-hand states.
- `P4 / S129–S160`: green optical-like, dark profile/object and threshold relation alternation. Operator, weapon, target and causal linkage are `UNKNOWN`.
- `P5 / S161–S200`: close 2D body overlaps, hands, compact/cable/tool-like object states and group views. Physical contact, transfer, procedure, hazard and success/failure are `UNKNOWN`.
- `P6 / S201–S211`: doorway-side reorientation and exterior door/step/platform/body states. Egress meaning and outcome are `UNKNOWN`.
- Across cuts, recurring visual aliases, same-space topology and chronology never rise above `INFERRED`; exact continuity remains `UNKNOWN`.

## E. Editing, rhythm and cut method

- Shot count: 211
- Total selected duration: 767.708333 s / 18425 frames
- Mean duration: 3.638428 s
- Median duration: 2.875000 s
- Minimum: 0.625000 s (BODYGUARD-W5-S154)
- Maximum: 19.375000 s (BODYGUARD-W5-S206)
- Distribution: <1.0 s = 9; 1.0–<2.0 s = 52; 2.0–<4.0 s = 77; 4.0–<8.0 s = 59; >=8.0 s = 14
- These statistics describe this selected envelope only and do not establish a general pacing rule.

- Primary over-inclusive local-max review produced 291 candidates; all were visually inspected. Seventy-nine were rejected as within-shot motion, occlusion or reframe, and 84 ambiguous cases received exact `F-2/F-1/F/F+1` review.
- An independent lower-threshold pass produced 136 additional candidates; all were reviewed and rejected, with no added cut.
- Every resulting shot received start/mid/end-1 picture review: 211/211 shots, 633 exact samples. Detector scores were navigation aids only; visible picture discontinuity determined acceptance.
- Accepted picture boundaries: 212 total = selected-in + 210 internal cuts + selected-out. Hidden/sub-frame joins, auditory edit points and production method remain `UNKNOWN`.

## F. Sound and performance grammar

- `SND-UNK` for every shot: the audio stream was not auditioned.
- Dialogue, negotiation, orders, calls/radio/PA, alarms, tool sounds, ambience, silence, music, impacts, off-screen cues and sound-led cuts are `UNKNOWN`.
- Gross head, gaze, mouth, shoulder, hand, body, object and spatial-state changes are picture observations. At 856×480, micro-expression, exact eye target and subtle contact cannot be established.
- Burned subtitles and watermark pixels were excluded from all visual and semantic claims.

## I. Evidence boundary

### VERIFIED FACT / OBSERVED

- Selected envelope: `[F12635,F31060)` / `00:08:46.458–00:21:34.167` end-exclusive; 18,425 frames and 211 gap-free shots.
- `F12635` is a visible hard cut into the selected wall/door-area registration and is an internal selected start.
- `F31060` is a visible hard cut from the retained exterior close to distinct road imagery and is excluded.
- Picture-only forms, positions and changes are recorded at exact shot/frame/time ranges.

### EVIDENCE-BASED INFERENCE

- Phase labels, visual-track/object grouping, cut motivation, narrative function, cross-node relation, chronology and stable coverage axis are interpretive and remain explicitly `INFERRED`.

### HYPOTHESIS / candidate rule

- `BODYGUARD-W5-C01–C04` are four transferable decision-logic candidates derived from this single source. Same-trigger counter-source testing is pending.

### UNKNOWN

- Full natural scene start; dialogue and every sound; names, roles, gender, exact identities and relationships; police/authority status; negotiation, commands, remote relation and simultaneity; explosive, medical, weapon or tool classification; ownership, physical contact, transfer, procedure, danger, cause, success/failure, rescue/arrest/release/egress meaning and outcome; exact lens, support and production method.

### Never copy into original work

- Do not copy source characters, faces, costumes, carriage or exterior location, props, image interface, signature compositions, subtitles/dialogue, or event sequence. Transfer only abstract, project-original decision logic after locked-script and safety review.
